# Government decisions (HG): 2021

1022 acts, 1,061 versions. Each act lives in a folder named by its official identifier, `<kind>/<year>/<number>/` (Legea nr. 31/1990 is `lege/1990/31/`); its README.md lists its articles and the decisions made by hand. Dates are in-force days. Generated on every publish; the same list is in [2021.json](2021.json).

Acts published here and later found not to belong (for example, titled as Government decisions but issued by another body).
